<Heading
  as={"h1"}
  className={"openapi__heading"}
  children={"查询自动化详情"}
>
</Heading>

<MethodEndpoint
  method={"get"}
  path={"/automations/{automationId}"}
  context={"endpoint"}
>
  
</MethodEndpoint>



查询自动化详情，支持通过自动化 ID 查询自动化详情。

按返回的 configFormat 判断 JSON_AUTOMATION / FLOW_CANONICAL；列表分类不等于更新资格。

自动化不存在或 ID 格式无效时，返回 HTTP 400。


<Heading
  id={"request"}
  as={"h2"}
  className={"openapi-tabs__heading"}
  children={"Request"}
>
</Heading>

<ParamsDetails
  parameters={[{"in":"path","name":"automationId","required":true,"schema":{"type":"string"},"description":"自动化ID"},{"in":"query","name":"includeMetadata","required":false,"schema":{"type":"boolean","default":false},"description":"是否回显对象用户 metadata。"}]}
>
  
</ParamsDetails>

<RequestSchema
  title={"Body"}
  body={undefined}
>
  
</RequestSchema>

<StatusCodes
  id={undefined}
  label={undefined}
  responses={{"200":{"description":"请求发起成功","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"allOf":[{"type":"object","x-business-success-condition":"code == 0","required":["type","version","msgId","code"],"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","description":"消息类型"},"version":{"type":"string","description":"接口版本","example":"v1"},"msgId":{"type":"string","description":"消息 ID"},"code":{"type":"integer","description":"业务状态码；仅 0 表示业务成功，HTTP 200 下的非 0 值仍表示业务失败"},"message":{"type":"string","description":"提示信息"}},"title":"BaseResponse"},{"type":"object","properties":{"data":{"nullable":true,"allOf":[{"type":"object","required":["automationId","name","enabled","definitionType","createTime","spaces"],"properties":{"automationId":{"type":"string","description":"自动化 ID。"},"name":{"type":"string","description":"自动化名称。"},"enabled":{"type":"boolean","description":"是否启用。true：启用；false：禁用。"},"definitionType":{"type":"string","enum":["SCRIPT_JSON","SCRIPT_TEXT","WHEN_IF_THEN","LEGACY_FLOW","LEGACY_LINKAGE","OTHER"],"description":"自动化类型，说明如下： <br />\n- SCRIPT_JSON：通过 API 创建的 Flow 自动化。  \n- SCRIPT_TEXT：通过 Aqara Studio Web 页面创建的 Flow 自动化。  \n- WHEN_IF_THEN：Classic 自动化。  \n- LEGACY_FLOW：Flow 自动化（旧版）。  \n- LEGACY_LINKAGE：Logic 自动化。  \n-
